One dispatch was issued by the master this session (`praxis-manager`, retrieval unit, brief bounded read-only at "find and report only" per Amear's `AskUserQuestion` answer). Amear stopped it and the worker it had spawned before either returned | WHERE: `DISPATCH_LOG.md:430-443`; intended deliverable `docs/reports/2026-08-29-session52-4uu-brief-retrieval.md` | WHEN: 2026-08-29 session 52 | HOW: **TERMINAL STATES, ASSIGNED FROM OBSERVATION NOT INFERENCE.** (1) `11:18:06Z` `praxis-manager` `corr=a8364a05f14e74c24` — **CLOSED**, keyed, at `11:19:01Z`; the token matches the agent id the master's own Agent call returned, so this pairing is solid. (2) `11:18:58Z` `praxis-worker` `corr=a20db6594c9e4e3db` — **STILL READS `STATE: dispatched` AND IS AN ORPHAN.** It is killed, not running; the row is not edited, per the session-37 precedent that rewriting stubs in place is itself an unaudited claim written over the primary record. **DELIVERABLE STATE, VERIFIED THIS SESSION AND NOT ASSUMED:** the report file was never created (`ls` rc=1); the index is **unmoved at 9 paths**, byte-for-byte the carried set; the only repo files touched since 06:45 local are `DECISION_LOG.md` (the master's own rows) and this file (hook-written). **THE READ-ONLY HARD LIMIT ON THE OUT-OF-REPO CORPUS HELD:** `find /Volumes/Sensidine/Hemres.Planner -newermt 2026-08-29T06:45:00` returns **nothing**. Nothing was created, modified or deleted in the other project. **THE UNEXPECTED FINDING, RECORDED AND DELIBERATELY NOT DIAGNOSED:** rows `:431` and `:434-443` are **eleven** `closed@unmatched-token` events, each carrying a distinct correlation token that matches no `dispatched` row anywhere in this log, arriving between `11:18:39Z` and `11:22:06Z` — **the last nine of them AFTER the manager was already closed at `11:19:01Z`.** The master issued ONE dispatch and capped the manager at MAX 2 workers, so at most three agents should exist; the log shows **two starts and thirteen stops**. **WHY THIS MATTERS BEYOND TIDINESS: hook-written rows are the master's ONLY evidence channel that a producer cannot author, and it is the channel sessions 32 and 37 used to verify worker caps.** If starts are being dropped while stops are recorded, the cap is unverifiable — and the master cannot presently distinguish that from a token-generation mismatch, from stop events belonging to a concurrent session (`CLAUDE.md` forbids parallel sessions against this tree; `HANDOFF.md` records five peer-seat relays), or from the closer firing per-turn rather than per-termination. **NO DIAGNOSIS IS OFFERED, BY STANDING RULE.** `ISSUE_REGISTER 2026-07-26T00:00Z`: *"never diagnose a deny-message mismatch by reasoning about hook order or payload shape. Instrument first."* The same applies here, and it applies harder because Claude Code moved to **2.1.251** this session and essentially no hook behaviour has been characterised on this build. **The cheap, safe, unrun test:** add an unconditional raw-stdin capture to the writer and the closer, spawn one agent, read both payloads. Not run — instrumenting a hook is a script change and is not the master's to make | STATE: closed by append — 1 keyed close, 1 orphan left standing unedited, 11 unmatched stops ESCALATED to Amear as an unexplained observation
